I mean, I still get up and go to work, and do what I need to do. I might feel a little drained sometimes just from being so stuck in my head, but... my actual energy levels and how active I am haven't really changed. I'm still keeping up with my normal routine.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having any trouble sleeping lately?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah, a little bit. I mean, I manage my projects at work without any issues, and I don't have trouble making decisions. I mean, I'm definitely tired from the lack of sleep, but I wouldn't say I'm sluggish or moving slower than usual. I still get up, get ready, and go about my day at a normal pace. It's just the being tired part, not really a physical slowness.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself feeling anxious or agitated, like you can't sit still?\n\nPatient: No, I don't get that feeling. I mean, my wife and kids can be laughing at dinner, or I can finally finish a difficult project milestone at work... and I know those things should feel good. It's... well, there's this constant, low-grade dread and guilt that just kind of follows me around now. I keep feeling like I should have seen one more risk coming with that scaffold. I mean, I feel pretty down and stuck in my head a lot of the time, but... no, I haven't had any thoughts about hurting myself or ending my life. I haven't really noticed any changes there.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your energy level or how active you are compared to before?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I mean, I still get up and go to work, and do what I need to do.
